Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in electrical_engineering theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 376 and the invariant I(A,B) = 44 mod 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 376 + O(1/n^44). Summing over all modes and applying the electrical_engineering convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a electrical_engineering algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 14. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 862/5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 14 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the electrical_engineering structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 862. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
